**This is the ONLY open-work list** (owner, 2026-09-12). Handoff memories, the
audit-bus ledger and agent reports are evidence, not backlogs: anything still
open from them gets a line here, with the check that guards it once one exists.

**Layout (reconciled 2026-09-23, Q16):** (1) the live QUEUE, verbatim, first;
(2) every item still open from older sections, one line each with its current
status and a pointer into the archive; (3) the 2026-09-02 ledger's leftovers
(docs/audit/OPEN_ITEMS.md was retired, then deleted 2026-09-27; history in docs/archive/OPEN_ITEMS-2026-09-02.md); (4) the audit bus, by
reference. History (closed sections, narratives) is verbatim in
[docs/archive/OPEN-history-2026-09.md](archive/OPEN-history-2026-09.md),
with a reconcile log of every item closed and its evidence. Nothing was deleted.

**Done items leave this file (Q16, 2026-09-26):** a ticked `- [x]` item is moved
verbatim to the dated archive docs/archive/OPEN-done-YYYY-MM.md by
`npm run inventories:refresh` (scripts/archive-done.mjs); check:generated fails
while one is left here. The queue score, next free number and the done-item
guard check read this file AND the archives, so archiving never changes a count.

**`[~]` items say when they are done (2026-09-27):** put what is left as one or
more markers on the item, written out in full and ANDed together:
``done-when: sql `SELECT <one column>` => <expected>`` (read-only, first row),
`done-when: test <path>`, `done-when: issue #N closed`, or `done-when: pr #N merged`.
Only add markers when they cover everything that is left (a screenshot or a
review still to do means no marker yet). `node scripts/open-done-when.mjs` runs them;
the nightly `open-done-when` workflow opens one nightly-red issue listing the items
that are ready to tick. src/test/openPartlyDoneItemsSayDoneWhen.test.ts ratchets the
count of `[~]` items with no marker.

**Priority among the open queue items (lead's order, 2026-09-23 — money and
security first, then alerting, then the audit, then hygiene):** Q53 (DB outage
root cause), Q50 (card holds on cancelled jobs, Stripe side), Q3 (Stripe test

- [ ] **Q7 WebKit only: the bottom nav isn't frosted. OWNER (device), re-checked 2026-09-26: still unmeasured in WebKit.** Nothing code-side can settle it. The dock is signed-in only (guest /browse renders no MobileNav, checked at 375), the cloud session that took this item had no test-account credentials, and Playwright WebKit on Linux is not the iOS compositor, so no CI run can show WKWebView frost either. What the source does (MobileNav.tsx:705-745): the full-width curtain carries `backdropFilter`/`WebkitBackdropFilter: blur(32px) saturate(170%)` together with `maskImage`/`WebkitMaskImage` on the SAME element, and the pill carries `blur(40px) saturate(180%)` inside the nav, whose own style has `transform` + `willChange: transform` (the hide-on-scroll slide). Both are inline styles, so the CSS minifier cannot have dropped the -webkit- form. **Ask:** on the iOS 26.1 simulator or a device, signed in, open /home, scroll the feed under the dock and send one screenshot. **Recommendation:** if the pill is frosted and only the band above it is clear, the curtain's mask is the suspect (test by removing `WebkitMaskImage` alone). If the pill is clear too, test with `willChange` removed from the nav. Fix whichever one screenshot proves, and add a pixel-variance check under the dock to a WebKit spec.

   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "cut it when main is current".** Claude runs fastlane beta from main

   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner asked "whats the best option?"): switch it OFF.** Measured: db-deploy
   36261725456 (18:16Z) judged 124 prod versions since the cutoff, 108 with a db-deploy receipt and 16
   acknowledged side-channel applies, the newest of them 2026-09-23 ~16:10Z (Q194). So nothing has come
   in by the side channel for about 3 days, but that is not proof the toggle is off. Off is the only
   setting where db-deploy's lint, replay and destructive-DDL checks actually run before prod changes.
   Still the owner's dashboard click; the provenance check stays as the detector either way.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

15. **Tax forms (W-9) after a Helpr deletes their account (CS-003, 2026-09-24).**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-24 (owner pop-up): keep 4 years after signing, then delete automatically. Work item Q370.**
   Deleting an account leaves the Helpr's W-9 record (typed legal name, signing IP)
   in place forever. The IRS expects a business to keep W-9s for about 4 years, so
   deleting them at once may be wrong too. Options: (a) keep them 4 years after
   deletion, then delete automatically; (b) delete them with the account; (c) keep
   forever. I recommend (a), but it is a legal call. No W-9s exist in prod yet.

   **ANSWERED 2026-09-24: same as MQ15, keep 4 years then delete (Q370).**

   privacy policy says tax records the law requires are kept, and the IRS says

   then delete them automatically; (b) delete them as soon as the account is
   deleted. I recommend (a).

   accountant which job types are taxable; (b) collect no sales tax. I cannot

29. **PR #1810 (Q392): which parish job-match alert design stays? (2026-09-26).** PR #1810 and main
    (20260926041132, Q225/V-008) both fix "parish alert arrives before early access shows the job", differently:
    (A) KEEP MAIN'S queue-only design (every send goes through the 1-minute sweep, so elite/no-delay users hear up
    to ~60s later; nothing sent inside the funding write) and ADD the PR's block check (`user_blocks`, both ways)
    and shared `job_announceable_to()` gate. A user who deletes the alert can be told again if the job reopens.
    (B) TAKE THE PR'S design: elite/no-delay users told inline at funding (incl. email via net.http_post inside the
    escrow write), a permanent per-(user, job) `job_match_queue` ledger so nobody is ever told twice, block check;
    main's `parish_match_alert_queue` and its sweep step are retired.
    Measured live (pg_get_functiondef, prod): neither `notify_helpers_on_job_post` nor `deliver_parish_match_alert`
    checks `user_blocks` today; `deliver_job_match`/`job_announceable_to` do not exist on prod.
    Not blocked by this: the PR's instant-match half, digest gating, export_my_data line, and Q401 (auth-page
    noindex) are being landed separately. The PR's old-timestamp migrations must never reach main as-is.
    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "A + never twice": keep main's queue-only send path, add the
    `user_blocks` check (both ways) and `job_announceable_to()` gate, AND write parish sends into the permanent
    per-(user, job) ledger so a deleted alert is never re-sent when the job reopens. Lands after the instant half.**
    **DONE 2026-09-26: landed as migration 20260926195608 (Q392 parish half).**

    (GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false), so nothing is charged under these yet. (A) CREW_FEE_PAYS_UNCONFIRMED:
    a late-cancel fee is shared by a member the poster OFFERED the job to but who never confirmed.
    Built as yes (your Q407(13) answer); confirm. (B) CREW_COMPLETES_WHEN_HIRED_DONE: an under-filled
    crew completes when every hired member is done, and the unfilled shares go back to the poster.
    Built as yes; confirm. (C) New: when a crew dispute or an unfilled slot returns a member's share,
    only the share itself (budget + urgent fee) goes back. The platform fee, sales tax and any gift
    card on that charge are NOT returned. Should they be returned in proportion to the share?
    ANSWERED 2026-09-27: yes, pro-rata. BUILT (see the ANSWERED block above); guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts.

FIXED: `supabase/functions/_shared/functionFetch.ts` waits the named `retryAfterMs` and retries (max 2 waits, never past a deadline: 60 s per call by default, and the three crons pass start + 120 s so they answer inside the 150 s gateway timeout); review-nag-cron, arrival-confirm-reminder, stalled-completion-reminder, create-notification, admin-test-push use it. - [~] **Q853 nightly-red #1966: stripe-webhook-guard now checks the LIVE account, read only (owner decision 2026-09-30).** Stripe went live 2026-09-27 and the test-mode endpoint is disabled on purpose (Q839), so the test-mode check was red every day. FIXED in code: the `live` job reads `STRIPE_LIVE_READ_KEY`, refuses anything but an `rk_live_` key before any request (sk_ keys can write), makes one GET /v1/webhook_endpoints, and asserts exactly one enabled live endpoint on the webhook url whose enabled_events equal EVENT_HANDLERS in both directions (measured 2026-09-30: we_1U5uqvKp2H4b7tECSZVlCM1Q, 15 types). Guard `src/test/stripeWebhookGuard.test.ts` (proven red on a missing event, an extra event, a second enabled endpoint, a test-mode endpoint, sk_/rk_test_ keys, and the workflow reading a test key). STAYS RED until the owner adds the repo secret STRIPE_LIVE_READ_KEY (restricted rk_live_ key, only "Webhook Endpoints: Read"). done-when: the next scheduled stripe-webhook-guard run is green and #1966 is closed.
- [ ] **Q854 restore undelivered-event detection (Q156) on the live webhook.** The live guard dropped the pending_webhooks > 0 check because GET /v1/events needs "Events: Read" and the owner's key is scoped to Webhook Endpoints only (assumed from the scope name, not verified against Stripe's docs). Options: add "Events: Read" to STRIPE_LIVE_READ_KEY and restore the check in `scripts/check-stripe-webhook-events.mjs`, or measure delivery another way (webhook receipt rows vs Stripe events). Also: `scripts/audit/expiry-inventory.json` tracks STRIPE_TEST_SECRET_KEY but not STRIPE_LIVE_READ_KEY.

## QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above

- [ ] **Q803 accept_application never stamps helper_confirmed_at, so "I'm On My Way" dead-ends on every apply→accept hire.** Merged 2026-09-27 from docs/audit/launch-2026-09/inbox/accept-application-never-stamps-helper-confirmed-at.md (deleted: OPEN.md is the one list). Re-verified on prod 2026-09-27 via `pg_get_functiondef`: accept_application never references helper_confirmed_at, helper_mark_on_the_way raises when it is NULL, and only claim_series_dates, respond_to_direct_offer and rpc_group_member_confirm stamp it. Fix: stamp it in accept_application's jobs UPDATE (or gate on `COALESCE(helper_confirmed_at, helper_dayof_confirmed_at)`), and have the E2E journey drive `helper_mark_on_the_way` instead of PATCHing the row. Re-measure: hire via apply→accept, tap "I'm On My Way", see the stamp land. STATUS 2026-09-27 (lane O3): PREMISE REFUTED, prescribed fix withdrawn (its done-when demanded it and is removed). The stamp is missing from accept_application BY DESIGN: accept_application makes the OFFER (status accepted, helper_confirmed_at NULL, response_deadline set); the helper's "Accept Job" tap stamps helper_confirmed_at (useOfferHandlers.ts client UPDATE, allowed by enforce_confirm_on_live_job + the helper column whitelist); expire_unanswered_offers reopens offers still unstamped at the deadline and auto_start_due_jobs starts only stamped ones. Stamping at accept would skip helper consent and disable offer expiry. Measured: live md5 accept_application 4524d9c68b84462743faf7eecbe8c634 (proacl postgres/authenticated/service_role), helper_mark_on_the_way a37e8d9b5d33e3834c9d4e2c335394ee; rolled-back prod probe on is_seed jobs (poster-e2e/helper-e2e): accept1=ok, accept2=ok, A helper confirm UPDATE rows=1 then helper_mark_on_the_way ok (A ends in_progress, confirmed, otw set); B unconfirmed -> helper_not_confirmed; 0 probe rows left. UI: an accepted-but-unconfirmed hire routes to OfferedActions (Accept/Decline), not the tracker, so no "I'm On My Way" is offered there; guard src/pages/jobs/appliedJobCard/appliedJobCardHelpers.test.ts, vacuity 2/2 killed 2026-09-27. Layers read: RPC (live defs), trigger/constraint (enforce_confirm_on_live_job, helper whitelist, expire/auto_start), client write (useOfferHandlers), reader (appliedJobCardHelpers, HelperTrackerPanel, JobTracking), deployed (live md5). OPEN RESIDUAL (why this stays [ ]): the 25/25 evidence came from e2e/prod-lifecycle.spec.ts (~line 908) PATCHing helper_on_the_way_at + status in_progress straight onto the row as the helper, and prod accepted it without helper_confirmed_at: 50 jobs have otw set and confirm NULL (all is_seed, latest 2026-09-26 14:14Z). So the confirm gate lives only in helper_mark_on_the_way and a direct REST PATCH bypasses it. Next: drive the confirm UPDATE + helper_mark_on_the_way in the spec, and decide whether helper_on_the_way_at should leave the helper whitelist (RPC-only), with a guard proven red.

- [ ] **Q444 A group job can be marked released while a roster member is unpaid (lh-money-escrow review of Q355 part 2, 2026-09-26, code read only, not reproduced).** supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers/transferCreated.ts flips jobs.payment_status to 'released' for ANY transfer that has a payout_transfers row, whenever the job is payout_pending/escrow/released, with no roster check. process-scheduled-payouts deliberately keeps a group job payout_pending until every roster member has a pending/paid payout_transfers row (the allRosterPaid block). So when one member's transfer throws ('Scheduled payout failed'), another member's transfer.created webhook can release the job; the cron selects payment_status = 'payout_pending' and never retries the unpaid member. Q355 part 2's money-held rule counts that state as still owed, so the alert stays open, but the payout itself is stuck. Fix: give transferCreated the same roster test (only the transfer that completes the roster may flip a group job), with a PGlite/edge test red on the current handler. Needs a money-escrow review.

No code change until then. OWNER 2026-09-27: there is NO CPA; owner: "whatever you found online will work". The lead researches Louisiana law from public sources (not professional tax advice) and decides (1)-(3). LEAD DECIDED 2026-09-27 (from public sources: R.S. 47:301.3, 47:340.1, the LDR reform FAQ, RSIB 23-001; this is not professional tax advice): (1) KEEP the Louisiana registration in Stripe Tax. Check that live taxreg_1TAZVsKp2H4b7tECyvJINQyg still covers state and local tax. ADD the same registration in TEST mode (the likely cause of Q441's $0), re-run Q441, and record the tax in cents. Ask LDR or the Remote Sellers Commission whether LH files as a marketplace facilitator (one return) or with LDR plus each parish, before the first taxable sale. (2) Taxable categories: assembly (fabrication or repair of tangible personal property) and handyman stay taxable. Handyman over-collects on real-property work, which is the safer direction. All other categories stay exempt. Follow-up build: add a 'work on a movable item?' flag so handyman on real property is exempt and carpet, rug and upholstery cleaning is taxed. Also fix the salesTax.ts header ('ten services' should be 11). (3) Service fee: CONSERVATIVE. On a TAXABLE job, the fee takes the labor line's tax code (create-payment ~640-650, plus the recurring precompute ~1446-1469). It stays nontaxable on exempt jobs. Tips stay nontaxable. This is a money-path build, reviewed with lh-money-escrow and stripeTaxCodes.test.ts updated.

- [ ] **Q446** (OWNER/DEVICE, OA-018 identity linking, 2026-09-25; supersedes Q359's steps): what an existing email account gets when it signs in with Apple/Google is now settled from GoTrue's source (supabase/auth linking.go + external.go, read 2026-09-25) and checked on our DB layer by `scripts/check-identity-linking.mjs` (db-smoke.yml on every migration PR, db-drift-detect.yml nightly on prod, rolled back): verified same email -> the SAME account (no second profile, profile data kept); UNverified email account -> the same account, GoTrue wipes its password and confirms the email (profile kept); Apple "Hide My Email" -> a NEW account that lands on /complete-profile; an unverified provider email -> refused with provider_email_needs_verification, now explained on /login (was "That page needs an account": ~/.lh-shots/oa018/before-375.png vs after-375.png). What no script can do is a real consent screen. Steps, each on web (Chrome) AND the iOS TestFlight build, with an address you control: (1) sign up with email+password, verify it, post one job; sign out; tap Continue with Google with the same Gmail -> you must land in the SAME account (the job is there; admin: `select user_id, provider from auth.identities where user_id = '<id>'` shows email + google). (2) Same with Sign in with Apple choosing "Share My Email" -> same account. (3) Sign in with Apple choosing "Hide My Email" -> a NEW account on the Complete your profile form (expected; see Q447). (4) Sign up with email+password and do NOT click the verify link; then Continue with Google on that address -> you are signed in to that account, and the password you set no longer works (Forgot Password restores it; see Q447). Record user ids and screenshots in the OA-018 note. Close with the four results, not before.
- [ ] **Q447** (OWNER DECISION, OA-018 follow-ups, 2026-09-25, from GoTrue's source, not reproduced on a device): (a) PRE-ACCOUNT DATA, **MEDIUM** (rated by the lh-authz-rls review of #1806, 2026-09-26): when an UNverified email account is linked (Q446 step 4), GoTrue removes its password and email identity to stop a pre-account takeover, but OUR profile row (name, phone, DOB, photo, city, bio, marketing consent, legal acceptances, referral) is kept as whoever typed it at signup. Wider than first listed (code read 2026-09-26): complete-signup's unauthenticated path (supabase/functions/complete-signup/index.ts ~200-269: account under 30 min old, never signed in, empty bio) also takes the avatar, license and insurance uploads, writes legal_acceptances and records the referral, and because it fills every ProtectedRoute gate field the victim never passes /complete-profile to see it; the attacker's phone becomes the contact shown to counterparties. GoTrue leaves the attacker no session, refresh token, MFA factor or phone identity (token.go refuses the password grant to an unconfirmed email; signup refuses email+phone together), and cleanup-abandoned-accounts deletes unconfirmed accounts after 30 days. Referral attribution should also require a confirmed email. If an attacker signed up first with a victim's address, the victim's Google sign-in lands in an account whose profile the attacker wrote. Options: keep (today), or clear the signup-entered profile fields when email_confirmed_at is first set by a provider link (no email identity left), sending the person back through /complete-profile. Clearing also costs a legitimate user who signed up and then used Google instead of the email link. (b) APPLE HIDE-MY-EMAIL makes a second account for someone who already has one; /complete-profile could say "Already have a Helpr account under another email? Sign out and log in with it" when the address is @privaterelay.appleid.com. Guard to name when done: a case in scripts/sql/identity-linking-scenarios.sql for (a); a /complete-profile test for (b).

Job 11c6aebe was an e2e-journeys fixture, COMPLETED, so the poster landed on the done bucket, which renders ALL their completed jobs; useCardExpansion.ts:70-74 auto-expands every completed job not BOTH tipped and reviewed (awaitsTipOrReview), and tipping is optional, so the test poster's 65 completed jobs (65 untipped, measured in prod 2026-09-26) all expanded; each expanded PostedJobCard mounts a JobTracking that runs its own job_tracking query (JobTracking.tsx:762-770, since fetchPostedActivityDetail pre-fetches tracking only for ACTIVE jobs, useActivityData.ts:258) plus its own realtime channel, and a helper avatar image (PersonTile -> UserAvatar). Candidate fixes (owner/UX call, not made): collapse completed jobs once REVIEWED (tip optional), cap default-expanded cards, and include completed ids in the tracking pre-fetch (or skip JobTracking on completed cards). STATUS 2026-09-27 (lane P3): OWNER DECISION 2026-09-27 built: useCardExpansion.ts opens by default only the newest MAX_DEFAULT_EXPANDED=3 completed cards still awaiting a tip or review (completed_at, else updated_at, else created_at); the untipped/unreviewed rule is unchanged and auto-collapse on tip+review still covers every awaiting card. (a) The capture needs the pending marker and the redirect path: a refusal still falls through to "That page needs an account" when sessionStorage is blocked, when the attempt is older than 15 min, or when GoTrue sends the error to the Site URL instead of /home because redirect_to is not allow-listed (GoTrue validates redirect_to against site_url's host and uri_allow_list; neither is in supabase/config.toml and I could not read prod's). Fix: when there is no marker but the URL carries GoTrue's `sb` fragment and an error_code from SOCIAL_AUTH_ERROR_CODES, capture it with provider-neutral copy; and have scripts/check-identity-linking.mjs assert that site_url / uri_allow_list cover https://www.louisianahelpr.com/home. (b) bad_oauth_state, bad_oauth_callback, flow_state_expired and flow_state_not_found are raised in GoTrue's callback middleware, which answers /auth/v1/callback with a JSON error body and never redirects back to the app, so the web copy for them cannot show: an unrecoverable screen the app cannot see (flow_state_already_used is not in the list either). (c) Apple's web cancel may arrive as error=user_cancelled_authorize with no error_code and show "Apple sign-in didn't work — give it another try?" instead of nothing (from memory, not verified). Guard to name when fixed: a case in src/lib/oauthRedirectError.test.ts per branch, plus the allow-list assertion in liveCheckScriptsFailClosed.test.ts.

- [ ] **Q572** (LOW, found working Q349, 2026-09-26, measured once): listing a Storage bucket as anon fails with an ERROR instead of a clean empty/denied answer. `POST /storage/v1/object/list/avatars` with the publishable key returned 403 `{"code":"AccessDenied","message":"permission denied for table jobs"}`, so a storage.objects SELECT policy reads public.jobs, which anon has no grant on. The refusal itself is correct (anon should not list avatars); the problem is that it comes from a permission error inside a policy rather than the policy's own condition, so any anon storage read that reaches that policy errors. Not read: pg_policies for storage.objects (no service role here). To close: find the policy with `select policyname, qual from pg_policies where schemaname='storage' and tablename='objects' and qual ilike '%jobs%'`, and scope it `TO authenticated` (or guard the jobs read) so anon gets a plain deny; add it to the anon-grant class guard.

- [ ] **Q550** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): a11y-webkit-prod.yml's sweep job signs in as the shared test accounts but does not hold the `prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts` job lock. Its two-engine matrix (chromium + webkit side by side for the diff) is why: one lock would serialise the engines, and GitHub's one-pending-per-group rule could cancel the queued engine. Its workflow group is prod-load on every trigger, so it never overlaps a scheduled suite or a press run, but a DISPATCHED split suite (journeys, prod-audit, slow-network, loading-states, privacy) can still overlap it. Fix: run both engines inside one job that holds the lock, as press-every-control now does (scripts/audit/press-wave.sh), then take its dispatch out of prod-load and remove it from DISPATCH_SHARES_GROUP. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (delete its NOT_LOCKED entry; the test fails until you do).
- [ ] **Q551** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): vacuity.yml's vacuity job holds shared-account credentials without the account lock. It runs on every push, and in the lock each push's guard proof would queue behind a 5-hour press run and be cancelled by the next push. It signs in only when a registered e2e guard's files changed, but then it drives the shared accounts beside whatever else is running. Fix options: move the credentialed (e2e) registrations into a separate job that holds the lock and runs only when such a registration is selected; or make those registrations use a dedicated seed account no other suite drives. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (its NOT_LOCKED entry).

- [ ] **Q721 OWNER DECISION: stale-deploy WebKit red (#1863) vs the Q178 first-paint budget** (measured 2026-09-26, local `vite preview` + `happy-path-webkit`). The 12 red tests in stale-deploy.spec.ts ("warm, guard armed … Try Again recovers" ×10, boot "stale HTML" ×2) all fail the same way: when a chunk fetch fails, WebKit caches the failure, and the recovery reload (`hardReloadBypassCache`, `_v=` URL) re-imports the same chunk URL from that cache without touching the network, so the page stays blank. The failure comes from Q178's `modulePreload.resolveDependencies` returning the deps list, which includes the chunk itself. Four variants measured:
  - `() => []`: **34/34 WebKit pass**, but `critical-path.mjs --check` gives 11 failures. The page chunk moves from round 2 to round 3, and readiness takes 7-8 rounds instead of 2, on /, /browse, /login, /signup and /posts.
  - `deps.filter(d => d !== filename)`: 10/10 fail.
  - Removing the `<link rel=modulepreload>` tags before the reload: 10/10 fail.
  - Keeping deps only when the importer is the entry or main chunk: 12 fail AND the perf budget still fails.
  
  In a real stale deploy the new HTML carries new hashes, so this bites only on a transient chunk failure (a network blip). **Choose:** (a) keep fast first paint and accept that Try Again can't recover from a transient chunk failure in Safari/iOS; the test would then expect a manual relaunch, which needs an owner decision because it changes what the test proves. (b) Recovery first: `resolveDependencies: () => []` and re-baseline the Q178 budgets (a slower first paint on phones). (c) Research a third way, e.g. a `Clear-Site-Data: "cache"` response on `_v=` reloads. Its WebKit support has not been verified.

- [~] **Q740** (nightly-red #1794 a11y-webkit-prod + #1654 loading-states-refresh, 2026-09-27): the pacer's single learned reserve. Capped at half the per-worker share (100 of 200), a list page's burst landed on a part-filled minute: a11y-prod-webkit busiest minute 439/400 (run 36263866330). Uncapped (0d0010181), one burst over the 200 share made EVERY gate wait for an empty minute, one page per minute: both a11y legs cancelled at the 60-min job timeout at test ~330/368 (36271541309) and loading-states cancelled the same way (36272891811). FIX: e2e/requestMeter.mjs learns the burst PER PAGE (gate key = path): a known page reserves its own burst, an unknown one min(half share, largest burst); samples carry `topBursts`, printed by the budget step. Guard src/test/requestMeterPacing.test.ts (8/8 mutations killed). 36218568850 (cancelled) never started a job: cancelled in the queue. OPEN until a dispatched run of each workflow completes green (busiest minute <= 400, inside 60 min, loading-states baseline re-prove passes). RE-RUN a11y-webkit-prod 36294688001 (main 0d2d66ebe, 2026-09-27): 368 passed, 0 skipped on both legs (skips cleared); chromium 38.3 min, busiest minute 371/400 PASS; webkit 40.5 min (timeouts cleared), busiest minute 437/400 FAIL. The breach is ONE page burst, not pacing: `/jobs/6e1a1c36-3d62-4b7d-8eea-fdbd233d52c2` (job-detail-completed) sent 330 requests on webkit (194 chromium) between two gates, more than the whole 200 per-worker share, so no gate can bound it. It is Q430's root cause: the poster's own /jobs/:id redirects to /posts?highlight=, the Done bucket renders all 69 completed cards and auto-expands every untipped one (JobTracking query + channel + avatar each). The test poster's residue grows every lifecycle run (160 cancelled, 69 completed, 16 in_progress). Secondary: the a11y job-detail-* tests never render job detail, they audit My Posts (same redirect). #1794 stays red until Q430 is fixed (owner UX call). The run's overall 'cancelled' is its fixtures job, bumped from prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts by press 36294859009; 36218568850 and loading-states 36294688957 were cancelled the same way (a newer pending request in the group cancels the older one). AVATAR CLASS (2026-09-27): the run's top repeated GET, `/storage/v1/object/public/avatars/:id/avatar.jpg` x719, was an APP defect, not the meter. UserAvatar set `src` before `crossOrigin` (React applies props in order), so WebKit/WKWebView started a no-cors load, then a second CORS load, and shared neither with the next <img> of the same URL (cache headers are fine: GET max-age=3600). Measured in Playwright WebKit, 40 <img> of one URL: src-first 41 requests, crossOrigin-first 1 (Chromium 1 either way). On a local build against prod /posts?filter=done, one Helpr avatar went 101 -> 1 request, all storage requests 95 -> 42 (evidence ~/.lh-shots/avatar-meter/). FIX: src/components/UserAvatar.tsx sets crossOrigin/loading before src. Guard src/test/imgCrossOriginBeforeSrc.test.tsx (every JSX <img> in src/, floor >30; plus rendered attribute order): red on origin/main and under its @mutate, green on the fix. The Q430 page burst above remains; re-run dispatched to measure what is left. done-when: issue #1794 closed STATUS 2026-09-27 (lane P3): nightly-red #1794 and #1654 still open at 2026-09-27T23Z; a11y-webkit-prod 36352716213 (head 2d0ac5ea) still waiting on the shared-accounts queue. Stays [~].
